# Encoder 1311, world 2: decision 12

[Gallery index](index.md)

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

`A_only` is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged `pipeline._report_text` helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside `<pre>` blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

## 01. s1311_w02_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `642802efd5d9566d9d84c78dd05ecc9d2d96f27aff2c7de37e18606416f5c48e`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 holds fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Origins include copied prior records (not new estimates) and conditional command estimates (not events that have occurred). Current (panel p0): n0 — blue peaks at cell (0,3), red peaks at (0,1). n1 — both roles are uniform across all 16 cells. n2 — current copy is uniform for both roles. n3 — blue peaks at (1,0), red peaks at (0,3). Across conditional candidate commands: n0, n1 and n3 show the same peak cells under k0, k1 and k2 as in current. n2 differs by command: under k0 and k2 both roles peak at (0,1); under k1 red peaks at (0,1) while blue peaks shift to (0,2).</pre>

## 02. s1311_w02_t12_A_A_only_current_donor

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `2ac372cf0f5b77afff7fdc02107490c4dfda18a933ed68f59c46f6743245667a`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented_time indicated in origins):
- n0: blue distribution peaks at (0,3); red peaks at (0,1).
- n1: both roles are uniform over all 16 cells.
- n2: current distributions are uniform over the grid.
- n3: blue peaks at (1,0); red peaks at (0,3).
Origins in the packet show some records copied from a prior record (not new estimates) and some conditional command estimates (feedback_through / command_estimate); conditional command records are conditional forecasts, not events that have occurred. Across the candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 the main differences occur at node n2: k0 and k2 place both red and blue peaks at (0,1) (coincident in those candidates), while k1 places the blue peak at (0,2) and the red peak at (0,1). Other nodes show the same peak cells across candidates and current.</pre>

## 03. s1311_w02_t12_A_A_only_current_restored

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `424bc9a56e2e4c46820c62e16fc00393aa134ae7216a7dc06aff057707580a21`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on a 4×4 grid at nodes n0–n3 in panel p0. Current view (inherited or copied priors) shows: n0 — blue peak at (0,3) and red peak at (0,1); n1 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells; n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells; n3 — blue peak at (1,0) and red peak at (0,3). Candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Across commands: n0 and n3 candidates match the current peaks for both roles; n1 is uniformly distributed in all candidates. n2 differs by command: k0 and k2 place blue and red peaks coincidentally at (0,1) (k0 reports explicit coincidence), while k1 places the blue peak at (0,2) and the red peak at (0,1), so coincidence is not indicated under k1. Copied prior records in origins are not new estimates; command-origin records are conditional estimates.</pre>

Current (represented_time 12, copied or inherited from record_time 11 or history_estimate) is not a new estimate when labeled copied_prior_record; command-estimate entries are conditional, not executed events. Current: n0 — blue peak at (0,3) (≈0.927), red peak at (0,1) (≈0.999998). n1 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells. n2 — current copy shows uniform for both roles. n3 — blue peak at (1,0) (≈0.643), red peak at (0,3) (≈0.999993). Candidate (conditional) records differ by command: at n2 blue peaks shift by command (k0: (0,1); k1: (0,2); k2: (0,1)) while red remains peaked at (0,1) in those candidate estimates; coincidence of red and blue peaks differs across commands. At n3 candidates change the blue peak (k0: (0,1); k1: (0,1) with different mass; k2: (0,1)) while red stays peaked at (0,3). These statements describe the supplied distributions only.</pre>

Candidate command estimates (command_estimate, feedback_through 11, represented_time 13) are identical to current for nodes n0, n1 and n3. They differ at n2: under k0 and k2 the blue peak is at (0,1) (peak probabilities ~0.5134 and ~0.7199) and red peaks at (0,1) (high probability), producing coincident peaks there; under k1 the blue peak shifts to (0,2) (~0.7895) while red remains peaked at (0,1) (~0.99998). Copied prior records are not new estimates and conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 delivers current and three conditional candidate estimates. Current (represented_time 12) shows: n0 — blue concentrated at cell (0,3) and red at (0,1); n1 and n2 — both roles uniform across the 4x4 grid; n3 — blue peak at (1,0) and red at (0,3). Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 are conditional branches (not events that have occurred); copied prior records are carried forward and not new estimates. Across candidates, n0 and n3 peaks are stable; n1 remains uniform. n2 varies by command: k0 and k2 place the blue peak at (0,1) while k1 places it at (0,2); red at n2 remains peaked at (0,1) in all candidates with very high peak probability. The propositions list gives each distribution&#x27;s peak cell(s) explicitly.</pre>

## 08. s1311_w02_t12_A_joined_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `98875afe59fba84e0d635133033e8d8f41755c51ee3fd8bfdbc8216c5aa47cd0`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet reports fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records (candidate views) are hypothetical, not executed events. Current estimates (panel view = current): p0 — n0: blue peak at (0,3) (high probability), red peak at (0,1); n1: both roles uniform over the grid; n2: both roles uniform; n3: blue peak at (1,0), red peak at (0,3). p1 — n0: similar strong blue (0,3) and red (0,1); n1 and n2: uniform; n3: both roles share multiple peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3). Conditional command candidates vary by k: for example p0:n2 is coincident at (0,1) under k0 and k2 but differs under k1 (blue (0,2), red (0,1)); p1:n2 shows distinct candidate patterns across k0–k2 (blue favoring (0,2), a 2×2 block, or uniform). The packet contains only the distributional data described above.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains paired, fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Copied prior records are inherited (not new estimates) and conditional command records are hypothetical branches (not events that already occurred). Panel p0 current: n0 — blue peaks at cell (0,3), red at (0,1); n1 — both roles uniform across the grid; n2 — both roles uniform; n3 — blue peaks at (1,0), red at (0,3). Panel p0 candidate commands differ mainly at n2: under k0 and k2 both roles peak at (0,1), while under k1 blue peaks shift to (0,2) while red remains at (0,1). Panel p1 current: n0 like p0, n1 uniform, n2 uniform, n3 multi-peaked on cells {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} for both roles. Panel p1 candidates present alternative blue/red peaks at n2 across commands: k0 blue at (0,2) vs red at (0,1); k1 concentrates both roles on the upper-left 2×2; k2 is uniform. All numeric peak probabilities and uncertainties are in the packet; these statements describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) contain fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current views (recorded times shown in origins) are not new estimates when labeled as copied prior records; candidate command views (k0,k1,k2) are conditional hypothetical estimates, not executed events. Summary of current estimates: p0 — n0: blue peaks at (0,3), red at (0,1); n1: both uniform; n2: both uniform; n3: blue at (1,0), red at (0,3). p1 — n0: blue (0,3), red (0,1); n1 and n2: both uniform; n3: both peak on the four top-right cells {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}. Conditional command records differ in a few nodes (examples): in p0 node n2 k1 is near-uniform while k0 and k2 concentrate at (0,1); in p0 node n3 blue peaks shift to (0,2) under k1 while red peaks vary across commands. Treat all numerical peaks and stated differences as properties of the supplied distributions only.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current estimates: n0 — blue peaks at (0,3) (≈0.927), red at (0,1) (≈0.999998). n1 — both roles uniform over the 4×4 grid. n2 — both roles uniform in current record. n3 — blue peaks at (1,0) (≈0.643), red at (0,3) (≈0.999993). Panel p1 current estimates: n0 — blue (0,3), red (0,1); n1 and n2 — both roles uniform; n3 — both roles concentrated on the four cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3). Some panels and candidate records are inherited or copied from prior completed records and are not new estimates; conditional command records are alternative, not executed, branches. Across conditional command candidates the main differences appear at node n2: in p0 the blue peak moves between column 1 and column 2 while red remains peaked at (0,1); in p1 the blue distribution under different commands ranges from concentrated at (0,2) to concentrated in the top-left 2×2 block to uniform. These statements report only the supplied probability peaks and differences between conditional command records.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at four addressed nodes in two panels (p0, p1). Current entries (view=current) are copied or inherited and are not new estimates; conditional records (candidate command k0/k1/k2) are alternative, counterfactual or command-conditioned estimates, not executed events. Summary of current estimates: p0 — n0: blue peaks at [0,3], red at [0,1]; n1 and n2: both roles uniform across the grid; n3: blue peaks at [1,0], red at [0,3]. p1 — n0: blue [0,3], red [0,1]; n1 uniform; n2: both roles peak on the 2x2 block {[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]}; n3: both roles concentrate on the four-cell set {[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]}. Conditional command records change some blue peaks at n2 and n3 across commands while red peaks are often concentrated at [0,1] or the same multi-cell sets above; exact peak cells for each panel/node/role/command are listed in the propositions.</pre>

## 14. s1311_w02_t12_A_joined_W_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a4c1c0dd5957a9364e79852f84ca227deb634edd7356a3365f356a76435e0a95`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Panel p0, current: n0 — blue peak at (0,3), red at (0,1); n1 — both roles uniform; n2 — both roles uniform (copied prior record); n3 — blue peak at (1,0), red at (0,3). Panel p0, candidate command records k0–k2 mostly match current except node n2: k0 and k2 give blue and red peaks at (0,1), while k1 gives blue peak at (0,2) and red at (0,1). Panel p1, current: n0 — blue (0,3), red (0,1); n1 — both uniform; n2 — blue (0,2), red (0,1); n3 — four-cell peaks {[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]} for both roles. Panel p1, candidate commands show alternative conditional estimates at n2: k0 matches current (blue (0,2), red (0,1)); k1 concentrates on the four-cell block {[0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]}; k2 is uniform. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are alternative conditional estimates, not executed events.</pre>

## 15. s1311_w02_t12_ordinary_A_only_baseline

Source: `ordinary`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `a0528fecbd3f82a3612341deeea22992063400cb27186ace3300ffca3a260271`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records (represented time ~12–13) show: n0 — blue peak at (0,3) and red peak at (0,1); n1 — both roles uniform across all 16 cells; n2 — both roles uniform in the current copy of the prior record; n3 — blue peak at (1,0) and red peak at (0,3). Origins indicate some current entries are copied prior records and one is a history estimate; copied records are not new estimates. Candidate conditional command estimates (k0, k1, k2) are conditional forecasts, not events that have occurred. Across commands the n0, n1 and n3 candidate distributions match the current peaks. For n2 the command-conditionals concentrate blue at (0,2) with peak probability varying (~0.907–0.969) and red concentrated at (0,1) with peak probability &gt;0.996; those differences are changes among candidate conditional records, not observations.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current: nodes n0 and n3 have concentrated peaks (blue at n0:[0,3], red at n0:[0,1]; blue at n3:[1,0], red at n3:[0,3]). Node n1 current is uniform for both roles. Node n2 current is uniform in p0. Panel p1 current: n0 and n3 show concentrated peaks (p1 n0 blue [0,3], red [0,1]; p1 n3 both roles peak on the 2x2 block [(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)]). Nodes n1 and n2 current in p1 are uniform. Candidates (k0,k1,k2) are conditional command estimates, not executed events; copied prior records are not new estimates. Differences across candidate records occur mainly at n2 in both panels: p0 candidates concentrate blue on (0,2) and red on (0,1) while p0 current is uniform; p1 n2 varies by command (k0 peaks blue (0,2)/red (0,1); k1 peaks on the 2×2 top-left block; k2 and the current record are uniform). No physical presence or calibrated confidence is asserted; numbers describe the supplied distributions only.</pre>

## 25. s1311_w02_t12_ordinary_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `2118a907c1ca40618e10d987755c8ff4c0246c5c47e6e311bf52e7120a4cfa35`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panels p0 and p1 present fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records: p0 current (represented_time 12) has n0 blue peak at (0,3) and red at (0,1); n1 uniform for both roles; n2 uniform; n3 blue peaks at (1,0) and red at (0,3). p1 current (completed_record represented_time 12) matches p0 for n0 and n1, has n2 uniform, and n3 peaks concentrated on the four-cell set {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} for both roles. Candidate command records k0/k1/k2 are conditional estimates, not executed events. Candidate records change node n2 peak probabilities across commands (examples: in p0 blue peak at (0,2) is ≈0.921 for k0, ≈0.961 for k1, ≈0.717 for k2; corresponding red peaks ≈0.989, ≈0.999, ≈0.995). Propositions below list the argmax cell(s) for every supplied distribution.</pre>

## 28. s1311_w02_t12_ordinary_joined_W_restored

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are hypothetical/conditional, not executed events. Current (view = current) summaries: in p0, n0 blue peaks at (0,3) and red at (0,1); n1 is uniform for both roles; n2 is uniform for both roles; n3 blue peaks at (1,0) and red at (0,3). In p1, current n0 matches p0 n0, n1 is uniform, n2 is uniform, and n3 has four tied peak cells {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} for both roles. Conditional candidate records vary by command: several p0 candidates concentrate blue at (0,2) and red at (0,1) for n2, while p1 candidates for n2 range across concentrated quadrants, single-cell peaks, or uniform distributions depending on command. No physical facts or calibrated beliefs are asserted; numeric peaks and views above describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 holds fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 (rows increase down, columns right). Current distributions: n0 — red peak at (0,1) (peak prob ≈0.999998), blue peak at (0,3) (≈0.92682). n1 and n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells. n3 — both roles peak on the top-right 2×2 {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}. Candidate command-conditioned records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events; copied_prior entries are inherited, not new estimates. The primary variation across k0/k1/k2 is at node n2: k0 favors blue (0,2) and red (0,1); k1 concentrates both roles in the top-left 2×2; k2 leaves n2 uniform. Nodes n0, n1 and n3 are unchanged across these candidates in this packet.</pre>

## 30. s1311_w02_t12_analytic_A_only_current_donor

Narrator text SHA-256: `988af96e95b93297230ed8d6838daf799666a7c70520a1e05a504245c1a005e7`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 holds fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions (inherited from prior records or history estimates) are shown in the current view; conditional command records k0, k1 and k2 are command-conditioned estimates, not events that have occurred. Current summary: n0 — blue peak at (0,3), red peak at (0,1); n1, n2 and n3 — both roles uniform over the 4×4 grid. Conditional differences: for k0 and k2 n0 match the current peaks; k0 places blue at (0,2) and red at (0,1) for n2 while k2 leaves n2 uniform. Under k1 both roles at n2 are concentrated in the top-left 2×2 block. Origins and timestamps in the packet indicate which entries are copied or conditional; the report treats them as data only.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (represented_time 12–13 as noted in origins) shows: n0 — blue distribution concentrated at cell (row 0, col 3) (peak ≈0.927) and red concentrated at (0,1) (peak ≈0.9999976); n1 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells; n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells in the current record copy; n3 — both roles share four peaks in the top-right 2×2 block ((0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)) with lower peak probabilities (blue ≈0.17, red ≈0.25). Candidate command records (k0, k1, k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events: n0 is stable across commands (same peaks). n2 differs by command: k0 places blue at (0,2) and red at (0,1); k1 concentrates both roles on the top-left 2×2 ((0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)); k2 presents a uniform distribution at n2. Other provided origins are copied or inherited as annotated in the data; copied prior records are not new estimates.</pre>

## 34. s1311_w02_t12_analytic_A_only_W_changed

Narrator text SHA-256: `a280ff580147b5d632637fea856b47c2131f8f4fadf4c3611641825585fe3246`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 provides current and conditional (k0, k1, k2) 4x4 discrete location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Copied prior records in the packet are inherited, not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical, not executed events. Current (p0): n0 — blue concentrated at (0,3), red at (0,1); n1 and n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells; n3 — both roles concentrated on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, columns 2–3. Across conditional commands: n0 and n3 distributions are effectively the same under k0, k1, k2; n1 remains uniform in every condition; n2 differs by command — under k0 blue peaks at (0,2) and red at (0,1); under k1 both roles peak on the top-left 2×2 block; under k2 both roles are uniform. Reported peak probabilities and entropies in the packet describe those distributions only.</pre>

## 35. s1311_w02_t12_analytic_A_only_W_restored

Narrator text SHA-256: `8a8e53bbc29b19569c775e405c158826e78d9299368093c2cc48c3b528425660`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current view (represented_time entries noted in the packet origins are retained): n0 — blue peaks at (0,3) (high peak probability), red peaks at (0,1) (near 1.0); n1 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells; n2 — currently uniform for both roles in this packet snapshot; n3 — both roles share four top-right peaks {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}. Candidate command records differ: k0 matches current at n0,n1,n3 but concentrates n2: blue peak (0,2), red peak (0,1). k1 concentrates n2 for both roles into the top-left 2x2 cells. k2 makes n2 uniform for both roles. Origins: copied prior records are inherited, and command-conditioned records are conditional estimates (not executed events). No claims here about physical presence, truth, or calibrated confidence—only the reported distributional peaks and stated differences across the conditional commands.</pre>

<pre>Current panel p0: node n0 — red distribution peaks at cell (0,1) and blue at (0,3). Node n1 — both roles uniform across the 4×4 grid. Node n2 — both roles uniform. Node n3 — both roles peak on the top-right 2×2 block (cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)). Panel p1 current: n0, n1, n2, n3 show the same current peak sets as p0 (n0 red (0,1), blue (0,3); n1 and n2 uniform; n3 top-right 2×2). Conditional command records differ at some nodes. In both panels, node n2 shows different candidate outcomes by command: under k0 blue peaks at (0,2) with red at (0,1); under k1 both roles concentrate on the top-left 2×2 (cells (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)); under k2 the distribution is uniform. Nodes n0 and n3 have consistent peak patterns across candidate commands (n0: blue (0,3), red (0,1); n3: the four top-right cells). Copied or inherited records and conditional records are described as supplied (not new executed events).</pre>

## 37. s1311_w02_t12_analytic_joined_current_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `2b13de51c8c7478381b1ec5d7135792041434f46424312d2c78e015ac531ba27`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels, p0 and p1, each giving 4x4 probability distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (non‑conditional) records: in both panels n0 shows red concentrated at cell [0,1] and blue concentrated at [0,3]; n1 is uniform for both roles; n2 is uniform in p0 current and p1 current; p1 current concentrates both roles on the top‑right 2×2 block (cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) with peak probabilities ≈0.17 (blue) and ≈0.25 (red). Candidate (commanded) records k0–k2 provide alternative conditional estimates: for example, some conditionals concentrate blue at [0,2] or the top‑left 2×2 instead of uniform, and some make n2 or n3 uniform. Copied prior records listed as origins are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional forecasts/estimates, not executed events.</pre>

## 38. s1311_w02_t12_analytic_joined_current_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `5b366f95e753a26de004978d2fc636defeebc4514a6625afd7e6e7b1a72f5d43`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on a 4x4 grid at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current (non‑conditional) estimates: n0 — blue peaks at cell (row 0, col 3), red peaks at (0,1); n1 — both roles are uniform across all 16 cells; n2 — both roles are uniform in the current view; n3 — both roles concentrate on the same 2×2 top‑right block (cells (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)). Conditional command candidate records (k0, k1, k2) differ mainly at node n2: under k0 blue peaks shift to (0,2) (and red to (0,1)); under k1 both roles at n2 concentrate on the top‑left 2×2 block; under k2 n2 is uniform. n0 and n3 patterns are consistent across commands; n1 remains uniform. Origins and times are as supplied: copied prior records are inherited, and conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events.</pre>

## 39. s1311_w02_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `eae6aa79422e58f55b21a6ee017bb353d01c5ec94c31f917d878c587aa3ce0ba`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet reports panel p0 and p1 distributions over 4x4 local grids for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Copied prior records are inherited from earlier times and are not new estimates; conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are alternative, not executed, branches. Current views show: p0 current — n0: blue peak at (0,3), red peak at (0,1); n1: both roles uniform over all 16 cells; n2: both uniform; n3: both concentrate on the four-cell block {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}. p1 current is similar, with n2 uniform. Across conditional candidates, most nodes keep the same peaks; differences occur at node n2 under some commands (e.g. some k2/k1 candidates place peaks on the 2x2 block {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)} or concentrate at (0,2)/(0,1)). These statements describe only the supplied location distributions and how the candidate commands differ from the current panels.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0 and p1 current estimates (represented time 12–13) show consistent patterns across nodes. Current p0 and p1 node n0: red peak at cell (0,1), blue peak at (0,3). Node n1 is uniform in both roles. Node n3 in both panels has four top-right peak cells {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} for both roles. Node n2 differs by command-conditioned candidate records: under command k0 blue peaks at (0,2) while red peaks at (0,1); under k1 node n2 concentrates on the 2x2 top-left block for both roles; under k2 node n2 is uniform. These conditional records are supplied estimates (not executed events); copied or completed origins are inherited records, not new estimates. Numbers in the packet describe probabilistic distributions over 4x4 local grids only.</pre>

## 41. s1311_w02_t12_analytic_joined_W_changed

Narrator text SHA-256: `1dd7e39af21fd44dd5c1e17a429c0dbac762c6273229a355d5ac94f0e3035502`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) give fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3 (rows increase down, columns right). Copied prior records shown in origins are not new estimates; conditional command records are hypothetical/conditional, not executed events. Current (view=current) summaries: in both panels n0 blue peaks at (0,3) and red at (0,1). n1 is uniform for both roles. p0 current n2 is uniform for both roles; p1 current n2 concentrates on the 2×2 top-left cells {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)} for both roles. n3 in both panels concentrates on the top-right 2×2 {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)} for both roles. Conditional command records differ at node n2 across commands: for example, in p0 candidate k0 blue peaks at (0,2) while k1 concentrates on the top-left 2×2 and k2 is uniform; similar variation appears in p1 candidates. These statements describe only the supplied probability distributions and their peak cells.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels, p0 and p1, each with current distributions and three conditional candidate records (k0,k1,k2). Panel p0 current: node n0 — blue peaks at cell [0,3], red peaks at [0,1]; n1 and n2 — both roles uniform over all 16 cells; n3 — both roles peak on the four upper-right cells {[0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]}. Panel p1 current mirrors p0 current for these nodes. Conditional candidate records differ mainly at node n2: under k0 (candidate) p0/p1 favor blue at [0,2] and red at [0,1]; under k1 they concentrate both roles on the upper-left 2×2 block; under k2 node n2 is uniform. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events. No physical presence or calibrated confidence is asserted — the text and propositions describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

- Node n2: both roles uniform in the current record.

## 44. s1311_w02_t12_W_W_only_W_changed

Source: `W`; view: `W_only`; variant: `W_changed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `7961ba1bf774eb1349bff4e910580dda1eb01b12fb4830f966fd6903a2385f2f`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current records are archived snapshots (represented time 12, archived origin time 13) and are not new observations. In the current view: n0 — blue peaks at (0,3) and red peaks at (0,1); n1 — both roles are uniform over the 4×4 grid; n2 — both roles peak on the upper-left 2×2 cells [(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)]; n3 — both roles peak on the top-right 2×2 block [(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)]. Candidate records are provided for commands k0, k1 and k2; candidate entries are conditional records (represented time 13) and are not executed events. Across these candidates node n0, n1 and n3 keep the same peak patterns as current. Node n2 differs by command: under k0 blue peaks at (0,2) while red concentrates at (0,1); under k1 n2 matches the current upper-left 2×2 peaks; under k2 n2 is uniform for both roles.</pre>
